# PROJECTS — the cockpit

> **Read this FIRST, at the start of every session.** It is the single air-traffic-control view of every active initiative: where each one stands, what to do next, and — most importantly — **what each one is blocked on**. You run many fronts in parallel and lose context when you switch; this file is the antidote. You should never have to remember "where was I?" — it's here.
>
> **This file is agent-maintained.** Per CLAUDE.md §0.5 (Project Ledger Protocol), the agent surfaces the relevant row when you return to a project (check-in) and updates the row + the project's detail doc + GBrain when work advances (check-out). If you ever find a row stale, that's a bug in the ritual — tell the agent to fix it.
>
> Status legend: 🟢 active/shipping · 🟡 in progress · ⛔ blocked (waiting on a dependency) · 💤 idle (untouched a while) · ✅ done. **"Next action" is always the single next concrete step.**

_Last reviewed: 2026-07-25_

Measured: `elementFromPoint` at the handle centre returned the decoration and **19 of 41 px across the handle were dead**; after the fix the hit target resolves at centre with **0 dead px**, and a synthetic press dead-centre drags correctly (90°→120°, |R| 8.5→6.0 N). One shared component, so it fixes **every `vector_board` and all of Vector Lab**. Swept the estate (218 sim files · 48 with pointer handlers · 16 with real drag): 9 further candidates, **all false positives**. The newer bench families (fbd/energy/circuit/optics/field/motion-lab) already paint the hit target LAST with guarded visuals and comment the ordering — `DraggableHead`, the oldest, was the only one backwards. **(b) Space waste — founder right, and worse than 75%.** Measured by running each archetype's own pure `build()` and taking the bbox of what it actually draws: **median ink coverage was 5% of the canvas** (not 25%) and **9 of 9 archetypes could be ≥50% bigger**. Through-line: *a motion in a plane is two one-dimensional motions happening at once and ignoring each other* — so every projectile result is Ch.2 twice, side by side. Step scripts are author-editable data (`block.steps`), with defaults per archetype. **(c) A drag-practice board now sits immediately after every teach board** (founder's explicit ask), with `lock_magnitude` so “swing B round” really only rotates it. **8 real defects found by driving the boards in a browser — none catchable by tsc or Zod:** (1) **boards were completely dead to the pointer** — drag handles were gated on `t>=1` so an animated board never became interactive, which is exactly the “Think It Through says drag but the board does nothing” bug; (2) **flicker while dragging** — the sidebar growing stretched the grid row and resized the `height:100%` SVG, so the goal ring appeared to change size; fixed with a fixed-px canvas + `items-start` (verified: ring radius and canvas size constant across 14 drag frames); (3) **success strobed** at the tolerance edge — now latches, and the latch moved out of the drag handler into an effect on the rendered match so landing on the goal in ONE motion also latches; (4) crowded formula → `formula` is now `string[]`, one line per row; (5) **`B` and `R` labels printed on top of each other** (in the triangle law the resultant’s tip IS B’s tip) → new `labelAt:'mid'`, and the identify diagrams detect coincident tips automatically; (6) the four “which diagram?” options were each auto-fitted to a **different scale** → one shared frame, because a comparison question must be comparable; (7) formula printed `(5.6 − −0.8)` → `minusTerm()` + a sweep of 80 rendered formula lines now guards it; (8) **a 550ms draw-in took ~2s and taps during it were dropped** → root cause was the `specs` memo keyed on block *identity*, which the autosaving editor recreates constantly, causing continuous re-seeding; now content-keyed, 30fps frames, memoised KaTeX panel, and a click during the draw **skips** instead of being swallowed. One table, six chapters unlocked, zero cost. **Recommended restructure: Ch.0 goes 17 → ~26 pages, of which 11 are NEW calculus pages** (Δ vs d · slope/secant→tangent · the rules · chain rule · 2nd derivative + six rates · maxima-minima + related rates · integration as undoing · substitution · definite+area · initial-value problems), plus a new **Function** page and a second **Trigonometry** page. **Only new code recommended: three new `practice_bank` ITEM KINDS** (`comprehension` via an optional section `stem`, `assertion_reason`, `match_columns`) rather than three new block types — one schema change + one renderer. Fixed the colors (indigo/amber) AND shipped a general **frame-sizing system** on `ArchetypeDef` — `frameAnchor`/`frameQuadrant`/`frameReach`, computed ONCE from static bounds (never the live drag position, which would reintroduce the flicker bug fixed earlier this session) — plus a new `show.compass` flag rendering EAST/WEST/NORTH/SOUTH on the axes. scalar-vs-vector's scale went **20 → ~43px/unit** (anchored bottom-left, quadrant-restricted to up+right since its vectors never go anywhere else). **A real bug was caught by hand-tracing the arithmetic before ever loading a browser:** the quadrant-restriction merge defaulted every direction to `true` and then spread the archetype's partial override on top, so unmentioned directions silently stayed `true` — with the new off-centre anchor this would have made the board SMALLER (scale ~10, floor-clamped) than before, the opposite of the fix. ARCHETYPE_DEFAULT_BOUNDS[archetype] ?? DEFAULT_BOUNDS` — so EVERY future page using `archetype:'unit-circle'` without an explicit override now gets a sensible view automatically, closing the class of bug rather than the instance. **Both verified live** in the isolated admin preview (driving the actual guided steps / dragging the actual slider, not just reading code): scalars-and-vectors now shows indigo+amber legs filling most of the canvas with compass labels; unit-circle now shows a large, sharp triangle (violet radius, amber sin-leg, emerald cos-leg) with a working live sin/cos readout. Fixed 2 CSS traps: `overflow-x:auto` on the book row silently clipped the hover lift (padding+negative-margin), and `justify-content:center` made the first book unreachable once the row scrolls (`safe center`). **Round 3 (2026-07-31):** **ROOT-CAUSED the books-won't-rotate bug — `filter: brightness()` was set on the same element as `transform-style: preserve-3d`. `filter` is a GROUPING PROPERTY: it forces `transform-style: flat`, collapsing all faces into one plane, so the book was squashing rather than turning.** (Same applies to `opacity`/`mask`/`backdrop-filter`. Dim the FACES instead — `.lb-front::after`.) Replaced the geometry with the founder's reference model: a true SIX-FACE box (front/back/spine/top/fore-edge) in a `.lb-scene` that owns the perspective, all faces derived from `--lb-w/--lb-h/--lb-t` so thickness still tracks page count; hover moved to the CARD so the hit area is a stable rectangle, not a skewed 3D silhouette. **Wooden plank DELETED** (founder: didn't look good) — books now float above glass cards in a responsive grid, per the reference. **Cloth brightened substantially** (books read dull/near-black against the lifted #121316 ground). Hero rebuilt: real brand lockup (mark + wordmark + live state) replacing the bare text pill, gradient `Class N`, and the 8 features listed as a two-column sentence instead of 4 coloured chips. Ambient aurora now 3 independently-drifting subject-tinted blobs. **Round 4 (2026-07-31) — cover-art + title fixes.** **Root cause of the bad covers: `bookImagery.ts` hardcoded `page_number: 1`, but chapter OPENERS are page 0** — so every opener was skipped and the second page's image used instead. Now does a cheap page-number-only pass, takes each chapter's true min `page_number`, then reads `blocks` for those openers alone (2 bounded queries, still behind the 24h ISR cache). That alone fixed Chemistry: it now shows its *"glass beaker on a dark lab bench with luminous particles rising into a constellation of atoms"* opener instead of a murky landscape. **Biology needed curation** (its own opener is a sunset seascape) → `books.cover_image` SET by hand to the ch2 *"bacteria, pond microbe, mushroom, fern and deer each glowing"* scene. A hand-set `cover_image` on the book doc now WINS over the auto-pick on all four grade pages, so any cover can be overridden without a code change. Physics has zero images in-book and stays cloth-only by design. **Cover titles were overflowing the 150px board** — fixed three ways: subjects are de-slugged + title-cased (`social_science` → `Social Science`), a stepped `coverTitleSize()` shrinks long names, and a `COVER_NAME` map prints the short form students actually say (**Mathematics → "Maths"**, founder call) while the card body/a11y keep the full subject. **Round 5 (2026-07-31) — density.** Founder asked whether to go single-row-scrollable; **advised AGAINST and they took it**: a horizontal scroller hides 2+ of 6 books behind an affordance students don't use, destroys the Core/Electives split (real information for a JEE aspirant), and doesn't save height anyway since the complaint was per-book SIZE, not row count. Fixed instead by shrinking: **book 150x214 → 112x158 (~74%)**, grid `minmax` 210→172px so 5-6 fit per row instead of 3, gaps 92→64px, cover furniture/type/card body all scaled to match (`coverTitleSize` rescaled for the new 85px usable board width). **Also fixed a real overlap bug the resize surfaced:** the book was 214px tall hanging 66px above a card with only 132px padding-top, so it reached 148px in and covered its own card title (visible as 'Mathematics' and 'Social Scien…' being obscured). Padding is now derived from the geometry — `height - overhang + 14px` clearance — and asserted: 158-58=100 into a 114px pad = 14px clear. Hero also toned down per founder: `Class N` 6xl→2.6rem and font-black→extrabold, tagline 2xl→lg and semibold→medium. **Round 6 (2026-07-31) — contrast + the real thumbnail bug.** **Chapter thumbnails were blank on most rows because `chapter_opener` pages carry an `image` block with NO url** — they're title cards still awaiting artwork — and the picker stopped at the opener. Now walks the first `CANDIDATES_PER_CHAPTER = 3` pages of each chapter and takes the earliest that actually yields a URL (ties broken by rank, since Mongo doesn't guarantee `$in` result order). Coverage measured before/after against the live DB: **Class 9 english 0→8/9, hindi 0→13/13, science 1/5→5/5, social_science 2/2, life_skills 1/1; Class 11 chemistry 3/3, biology 3/3.** Genuinely image-less: class-9 maths (0/2), class-9 english ch9, class-11 physics ch0 — those now render a `BookOpen` placeholder glyph instead of an empty box that read as a broken image. Also documented the second trap in that file: **page_number is BOOK-WIDE, not per-chapter** (class-9 science ch1 starts at -1, ch2 at 7, ch3 at 16), so 'first page' must always mean min-within-chapter. **Contrast pass:** 30 raw Tailwind `zinc-400/500/600` greys in the chapter rows, stats strip and search replaced with the white-alpha scale (mostly /55–/60 for content, /35–/45 for decoration) — founder reported them unreadable on the plum-tinted cards. **Grid pinned to 4 fixed columns** (was `auto-fit`, which stretched 3 books to fill the row) with `max-width: 238px` on the card so a 5th/6th subject wraps instead of re-inflating everything; book size deliberately unchanged per founder. **Round 7 (2026-07-31) — one ordered shelf.** Founder: with a fixed 4-col grid, Core (3 books at class 9) never fills a row, so adding 2 electives would push to a THIRD row. **Resolved by keeping the core-first ORDERING and dropping the two section headings** — the split only earns its space where it tells a student something actionable, which is true at class 11-12 (core IS exactly the 4 exam subjects) but false at class 9-10 (everything compulsory, core is 3). Because core still sorts first, class 11's first row comes out as P/C/M/B anyway — the grouping reads from order without spending a row on a label. Class 9: 6 books = 2 rows; +2 electives = exactly 2 full rows. Single `BookShelf` with heading 'Subjects'. **Caught the multi-row overlap trap before it shipped:** books hang 58px above their own card (`.lb-scene top`), so in a ONE-grid multi-row layout the second row's books reach into the row gap and collide with row one's cards (visible in the founder's screenshot). Row gap is now 112px desktop / 88px mobile — covers the 58px overhang PLUS the 14px hover lift with ~40px spare — and the CSS comment ties it to `.lb-scene top` so the two stay in sync. **Round 8 (2026-07-31) — selection state + the completion-colour ladder.** Founder: the selected book wasn't distinct. Active card now stacks FOUR cues (was one weak tint): lifted plum ground, bright rim, outward plum glow, and the subject name switching to `--plum-text`; `.lb-card.is-active:hover` (0-3-0) added so hovering the selected card still wins the cascade over `.lb-card:hover`. **Selection is deliberately PLUM, not the book's subject colour** — 'selected' must look identical on every card so the signal is learnable once; a per-subject selection colour would be decoration pretending to be state. **Completion ladder implemented (founder approved):** green was doing double duty for 'you read this' AND 'you answered correctly', so a student couldn't tell them apart at a glance. Now **plum = in progress (a page you've read) · gold = milestone (a chapter you've finished) · green = correct (quiz answers ONLY)**. 18 markers moved across `GradeLandingPage.tsx` (8) and `BookTableOfContents.tsx` (10); the only surviving emerald in either file is the `quizScore >= 80` badge, which is a genuine verdict, plus the hero's live-status dot. ⚠️ **Geometry is still UNVERIFIED in a browser** — tsc/lint/build clean, but the six-face maths needs eyes. Closed one real cross-engine gap: the student flashcards page (`FlashcardsChapterClient.tsx`) had NO url-param deep-linking at all (topic selection was pure client state) — added a `?topic=` read on mount so the mind map's CTA actually lands pre-filtered. **Content pilot: Mole Concept, 7 branches / 25 leaves**, all grounded in real material from the same-day flashcard gap-fill pass + question-bank solutions (never invented per Rule 0); every `flashcardTopic` value verified byte-for-byte against the real active `Flashcard.topic.name` values so every deep link resolves. Shipped as page 19 (`mole-concept-mind-map`, `published:false`) via a new insert-only script (`scripts/chem11-book/insert_mole_concept_mindmap.js`, mirrors the existing `_practice/insert_practice_pages.js` new-page pattern: raw `insertOne` + push into `books.chapters[].page_ids`, since `book-writer.savePage` is update-only). tsc clean across `data`/`book-renderer`/`student`/`admin` (one unrelated pre-existing `step_solver` gap noted in `admin`, not touched). **Renderer v2 (same day, after founder review of a mockup):** v1's radial layout was rejected — connector lines ran *underneath* the pills and radiated from the centre node's middle (because every path went centre-to-centre), and hardcoded positions let the leaf column collide with the branch column. Rebuilt as a **four-lane layout** (`leaf · branch · centre · branch`) with branches split left/right; since only one branch opens at a time only one leaf lane is ever needed, which is what lets four lanes fit a ~680px reader column. Lanes are fixed non-overlapping horizontal bands, so pill collision is now structurally impossible rather than spacing luck. Connectors are **DOM-measured** (`getBoundingClientRect` vs the container, re-measured each frame across the 320ms lane-shift animation and on `ResizeObserver`) and anchored to pill *edges* with a 6px clear gap, with all branches on a side sharing one trunk point on the centre pill's edge — measuring rather than hardcoding is what keeps the gap exact across container widths, font scaling, and future chapters' label lengths. Opening a branch shifts the whole map sideways to make room (animated). Added a **narrow-width (<560px) accordion fallback** so mobile gets the same tree without a squashed map. Display labels shortened to ≤21 chars to fit a 145px pill (`Empirical & Molecular Formula` → `Empirical formula`); the full-length `flashcardTopic` match keys are untouched, so every deep link still resolves — re-verified against live `Flashcard.topic.name` values after the change. Founder also considered 3D floating spheres (declined: WebGL bundle on every chapter page fights the §10 caching/perf discipline and punishes low-end Android) and hand-drawn sketch styling (declined for interactive controls — it hurts tap affordance and reads playful; the hand-drawn language stays where it already lives, in the generated illustration images). **Content framework v2 (same day, after live browser review):** founder tried the shipped map and flagged every leaf as "generic" — content was single-paragraph flashcard-style prose with the formula (where one existed) buried mid-sentence in parentheses, no bullets. For a calculation-heavy chapter that's the wrong shape: a revision map has to surface the usable formula/shortcut at a glance. Fixed at the schema level, not just content: `MindMapLeaf` gained an optional `formula` field (the one canonical equation/rule, rendered in its own highlighted KaTeX chip above the bullets — verified rendering correctly for both plain-text and fraction LaTeX), and `summary` is now always 2-4 short markdown bullets (rule/method first, a `**Shortcut:**` bullet where a genuine speed trick exists, closing on `**Watch out:**` where a real trap exists — NOT forced onto pure-reference leaves like "which reagent absorbs what," since an invented trap is worse than none). Also added `ul`/`li` styling to the shared `InlineMarkdown` component (Tailwind's preflight was stripping bullet markers platform-wide — this fixes bullet rendering for every block that uses it, not just mind maps). Rewrote all 25 Mole Concept leaves to the new shape (16/25 got a formula chip; the other 9 are genuinely conceptual). The framework is documented as a reusable authoring convention directly in the insert script's header comment for whoever builds the next chapter's map. **Verified live in the admin preview** (browser-automated: navigated the real editor, clicked through branches/leaves, read the rendered DOM) — confirmed formula chip renders separately from bullets, KaTeX fractions render cleanly, bullet markers show correctly, and the "Test yourself" href resolves to the exact real flashcard topic name (`?topic=Concentration%20Units` etc., URL-encoded correctly). — **2026-07-26 — Mole Concept gap-fill pass.** Re-audited the active `ch11_mole` deck (60 cards, not the stale "87" the taxonomy file claimed — corrected to 67 now) against the 332-question `ch11_mole` bank's real microConcept distribution to find genuine gap-testing holes (not padding): law-of-multiple-vs-definite-proportions confusion, mole-fraction-vs-molality denominator trap, gravimetric analysis (conserved-atom trick), excess-reagent leftover calc, gas-mixture ratio-swap trap, sequential-reaction reagent misattribution, stacked % yields. | **Structure Editor (ChemDraw-like)** | 🟡 | **BUILT 2026-06-14.** Admin authoring tool at `admin.canvasclasses.in/structure-editor` — a ChemDraw-class drawing canvas via **Ketcher** (EPAM, Apache-2.0, runs fully in-browser on its standalone Indigo-WASM engine; no backend). **Scope (founder, 2026-06-14): pure organic-structure DRAWING only** — no biomolecules (macromolecule mode disabled via `disableMacromoleculesEditor`), no SMILES/chemistry-data, no spectroscopy. Use case = redrawing blurred question-source figures into sharp, clean lines. Draws open/closed chains, rings, functional groups, stereochemistry. Export toolbar is image-only: **Download SVG** (vector, infinitely sharp), **Download PNG**, **Push-to-R2** (reuses the existing `/api/v2/books/assets/upload` route → hosted URL). SMILES/MOLfile/InChI outputs + the openchemlib preview were removed per founder; route First-Load dropped 580→230 kB. **All element symbols forced BLACK** (toolbar + canvas) by blanking ketcher-core's shared `ElementColor` map (no CPK colours). **Name→Structure SHIPPED 2026-06-14:** "Generate from name" sidebar input → admin route `POST /api/v2/iupac/name-to-structure` (proxies OPSIN @ EBI `…/opsin/ws/<name>.smi`, admin-gated, SSRF-safe fixed host) → `ketcher.setMolecule(smiles)` draws it. Verified vs OPSIN (benzene/2-methylbut-2-ene/cyclohexanone/etc all correct; gibberish→friendly error). **Structure→Name DEFERRED (founder decision 2026-06-14):** no free reliable offline engine exists (RDKit/OpenBabel/CDK don't do it; only paid ChemDraw/ACD/ChemAxon or best-effort AI) — skipped to keep the tool trustworthy. **2026-06-15 — 3 more features SHIPPED:** (1) **Dark/light export toggle** — "Dark page" recolours the exported SVG black→white + strips the bg so structures drop onto dark book/Crucible pages (Indigo only renders black, so recolour is done in-code on the SVG string); "Light/print" = black. (2) **Trace-over reference image** — load/paste a blurry source figure as a faint pointer-events:none overlay over the canvas (opacity slider + show/hide + clear) to trace a clean structure over it. (3) **Copy PNG to clipboard** + PNG rasterised from SVG at 2x for crispness. All export paths (SVG/PNG/Copy/R2) are theme-aware and selection-aware. **Crucible image-storage Q answered:** question figures already store per-question at R2 key `questions/<uuid>/svg/…` (single bucket `canvas-chemistry-assets`), Asset doc links `used_in.questions`, referenced as markdown `![](url)`; chapter lives in question metadata, not the path. Direct-attach-to-question (feature 3) deferred pending founder decision (full attach w/ question-picker vs hosted-link-only). **2026-06-15 — 2 more:** (a) **Reference image resize/move** — size slider (10–150%, default 60%), opacity, a Move toggle (drag to reposition, pauses drawing), show/hide, clear (fixes pasted images covering the whole canvas). (b) **Functional-group quick-insert buttons** (NO₂, COOH, CHO, C=O, CN, OH, NH₂, OMe, COOMe, CONH₂, SO₃H, Ph, F, Cl, Br, I) via `ketcher.addFragment(smiles)` — drops the group on the canvas to bond in. **Why buttons not Ketcher's native FG library:** the library's data files (`ketcher-fg-tmpls.sdf` etc.) are build-generated, NOT shipped in the npm package nor committed in epam/ketcher, and the demo CDN 403s — so Ketcher's Functional-Groups template tab is empty in our standalone embed (`staticResourcesUrl=""`). Self-contained buttons sidestep that. Files: `FunctionalGroups.tsx`, `ReferenceImageControl.tsx`, `StructureEditorClient.tsx`, `ExportToolbar.tsx`. **2026-06-15 (later) — FG buttons upgraded to NATIVE behaviour:** discovered Ketcher's full functional-group library (62 entries incl. NO₂ as a proper collapsed `SUP` abbreviation w/ charges + `SAP` attachment point) is **embedded in the ketcher-react bundle** (the un-shipped SDF is only an override). Extracted 20 common groups' authoritative molfiles → `functionalGroupTemplates.ts`; buttons now arm Ketcher's template tool via `editor.tool('template', {struct})` (struct from `MolSerializer.deserialize`, exposed through `KetcherApi.molfileToStruct`) → click button then click a bond end/atom = literature abbreviation + auto-snap + valence balancing (fallback `addFragment` still drops the collapsed abbreviation). Native Templates→Functional Groups dialog also works (embedded). Earlier plain-atom `addFragment(SMILES)` buttons replaced. Files: `apps/admin/app/structure-editor/page.tsx` (AdminPanel gate super) + `apps/admin/features/admin/structure-editor/{StructureEditorClient,KetcherCanvas,ExportToolbar}.tsx`; landing card on `app/page.tsx`; CSP `worker-src 'self' blob:` + webpack `jsdom/canvas` alias-false (Paper.js) in `next.config.ts`. Ketcher code-split behind `dynamic(ssr:false)` — never touches SSR or other routes. **`npm run build:admin` passes clean (exit 0).** **DEPLOY STATUS (root cause found 2026-06-15 via Vercel log):** on `origin/main` but **NOT live** — the `canvas-admin` Vercel build is **OOM-killed** (build container runs out of RAM building the heavy editor bundles Ketcher + Excalidraw); NOT a code error (lint/types pass, warnings only). Student app `canvas-classes-website` deploys fine. **Fix applied:** `experimental.webpackMemoryOptimizations: true` in admin `next.config.ts` (commit `5f11823`, on PR branch). **2026-06-19 — LOCAL-RUN FORK BUILT (founder chose the offline/local path).** Both editors ported into a standalone, NOT-deployed Vite+React app at `tools/canvas-draw-studio/` (lives outside `apps/*` + `packages/*` so it can never enter a Vercel build → the OOM blocker is sidestepped entirely; there is no production build). Required **3 Vite-vs-Webpack fixes** Ketcher needs (admin's Webpack auto-handled them): (1) `build.commonjsOptions.transformMixedEsModules` — ketcher-core's ESM build has a stray `require("raphael")`; (2) `define: { global: 'globalThis' }` — a Ketcher dep refs Node's `global`, which threw inside the engine-init promise and silently blocked `onInit`; (3) `optimizeDeps.include` of the ketcher pkgs for dev. | 2026-06-19 | **Founder smoke-test the local app** (double-click `Start.command`, draw + export in both editors). | **Anatomy Explorer (full-body multi-layer — "our Complete Anatomy")** | 🟡 | **VISION + ARCHITECTURE captured 2026-06-22 → [`_agents/plans/ANATOMY_EXPLORER_VISION.md`](_agents/plans/ANATOMY_EXPLORER_VISION.md).** Founder north-star: ONE sim showing the whole body with add/remove layers for every system (bones/muscles/nerves/vessels/organs…), as good as Elsevier Complete Anatomy, "no matter the time/effort." **The unlock:** Z-Anatomy is already a complete, segmented, TA2-labelled full-body atlas (CC BY-SA) — the single-organ sims (skeleton, heart) were the proof-of-concept. Architecture = per-system optimized glbs + **lazy-load** + Draco/meshopt compression + **LOD** + a unified `AnatomyExplorer` R3F engine (layer toggles, opacity peel, depth-peel muscles, tap-label, search, isolate, cross-section) + a structure metadata catalog (Z-Anatomy names + our plain-English/NEET blurbs). Honest scope: multi-month, desktop-first for the full view; won't 1:1 match Complete Anatomy's medical-grade fidelity/AR. Reuses our proven viewer rules + realism recipe ([[reference_3d_anatomy_simulators]]). **▲ 2026-06-22 — P1 RESEARCH SPIKE DONE → GO (build, don't license). Findings in vision doc §8.** Key results: (a) **no turnkey three.js web port of Z-Anatomy exists** (only a heavyweight Unity WebGL app) → we build the renderer either way, which kills the license case; (b) **perf budgets set from our 2 shipped assets** — heart 155K-tri/29-draw-calls/3MB, skeleton 302K-tri/38-calls/6.6MB; mobile ceiling ≈ <50 draw calls / ~150K tris / ~3MB resident, desktop ~300 calls / 1M tris → **whole body (9 systems) ≈ 270 calls/2M tris blows mobile ~5–13×, confirming lazy-load + LOD + draw-call-merge is mandatory**; (c) **compression SOLVED with zero CSP work** — meshopt shrinks skeleton 6.56→1.56MB (1.07MB over brotli wire), decoder ships locally in three, drei's `useGLTF(url,false)` already decodes it, node-name tokens preserved (draco is ~10% smaller but needs a CDN/self-host decoder → rejected); (d) **draft structure catalog built** → `packages/data/simulations/anatomy-structure-catalog.draft.json` (5,612 real structures across Z-Anatomy's 9 numbered systems, 1,944 TA2 collection names). | 2026-06-23 | **P2 STARTED — muscular system (first Explorer component, built on a new shared `AnatomyViewer` core; | **3D Anatomy Viewer (Live Books)** | 🟡 | **Flagship `heart-3d` STARTED 2026-06-21.** Strategy: harvest free CC-BY-SA anatomy geometry (**Z-Anatomy**, built on Japan's gov-funded **BodyParts3D** — already system-separated + TA2-labelled) and render with the **three / @react-three/fiber / drei** stack **already installed in `apps/student`**. Wedge vs Complete Anatomy / Visible Body ($35–75/yr horizontal med atlases): **NCERT/NEET-scoped, in-chapter, bilingual, near-zero marginal cost.** **Code shell SHIPPED (no Blender needed):** new injected simulator [Heart3DViewer.tsx](apps/student/features/anatomy/Heart3DViewer.tsx) (id `heart-3d`, registered in [BookReader.tsx](apps/student/features/books/components/reader/BookReader.tsx) `EXTRA_SIMULATORS` like `atomic-models`; **reuses the existing `simulation` block — no schema change**) with layer peel toggles + per-layer opacity + cross-section clip + click-to-label (NCERT blurbs) + graceful "model not attached yet" state; `.glb` uploads enabled ([r2-storage.ts](packages/core/r2-storage.ts) new `'model'` AssetType + books upload route, 60 MB cap). The `HEART_STRUCTURES` match-arrays ARE the Blender naming contract. tsc + eslint clean. Research briefs (asset/license + rendering/competitor + architecture map) produced this session. **✅ PHASE B MODEL BUILT + LIVE 2026-06-21.** Drove Blender via the bridge: downloaded `Z-Anatomy.zip` → appended the `Heart` collection (CC-BY-SA) → kept the 18 solid heart meshes (4 chambers, 9 valve leaflets, 4 papillary muscles, pulmonary-trunk stub; ~52K polys) → renamed to the `HEART_STRUCTURES` tokens → flat oxygenation colours (right=blue/deoxygenated, left=red/oxygenated, valves cream, muscle pink) → exported `.glb`. Two export gotchas solved: collections ship view-layer-EXCLUDED, and valves were parented to deleted FONT labels (un-parented w/ world-transform kept). Final 2.07 MB `.glb`, all 18 nodes named correctly, uploaded to R2 (`models/anatomy/heart-v1.glb`, CDN 200) and wired into the viewer (`HEART_MODEL_URL` set + `papillary` structure added). Build scripts in `scripts/blender/` (`blender_client.py` + `upload_glb.js` are keepers; `_*.py` are scratch). **✅ v1.1 GREAT VESSELS SHIPPED 2026-06-21.** The vessels are CURVES in Z-Anatomy; re-appended + un-parented (world-aligned with the heart, scale 1.0), gave each an anatomically-sized `bevel_depth` tube radius (aorta 0.013 … coronary 0.0035), converted to solid mesh via `meshes.new_from_object(evaluated)`, coloured by oxygenation (aorta RED, pulmonary artery BLUE, pulmonary veins RED, venae cavae BLUE, coronary RED — teaches the artery/vein exceptions). Model now **30 meshes / ~58K polys / 2.4 MB**, uploaded as `models/anatomy/heart-v2.glb` (CDN 200), viewer + `coronary_artery` structure added. Build script `scripts/blender/_v11_build.py`. **⚠️ CORS FIX 2026-06-21:** R2's public `pub-*.r2.dev` URL sends NO `Access-Control-Allow-Origin`, so three.js's fetch-based model load was blocked in the browser (showed the "loading soon" fallback) — images/video were unaffected (no CORS on `<img>`/`<video>`). Fixed with a same-origin immutable-cached proxy route [anatomy-model/[file]/route.ts](apps/student/app/anatomy-model/[file]/route.ts); `HEART_MODEL_URL` now `/anatomy-model/heart-v2.glb`. **Cleaner long-term option: enable CORS on the R2 bucket (Cloudflare dashboard) → point straight at R2 + drop the proxy.** **✅ v1.1 POLISH + BROWSER-VERIFIED 2026-06-21 (heart-v5.glb).** First browser test showed a "cabbage" — root-caused by ACTUALLY rendering (Blender Workbench renders + browser preview = the QA step that was missing earlier): (a) viewer was DoubleSide → open chamber shells showed interiors through the gaps = mush → fixed to **FrontSide default, DoubleSide only in cross-section**; (b) vessels had per-point curve radii of 22–30 → ballooned → **normalised all curve point radii to 1.0**; (c) ascending aorta sat at the world origin → **repositioned** between aortic valve + arch; (d) **axis mismatch** — chambers exported Z-up while converted vessels were Y-up (model bbox 1.43, heart shrank to a dot) → **baked every object transform to identity before export** so `export_yup` converts uniformly (bbox now 0.23, one cluster); (e) `onReady` inline callback caused a tag-effect re-render loop → **ref-guarded**. **Empty layer toggles now hidden** (panel filters to layers actually in the model → 4: muscle/chambers/valves/vessels). **"Follow a drop of blood" flow animation SHIPPED** — particles run vena cava→RA→RV→pulmonary artery→(lungs: flip blue→red)→pulmonary veins→LA→LV→aorta→(body: flip red→blue) with an 8-step narration caption. **BROWSER-VERIFIED in preview: heart renders correctly framed + recognisable, 4 layers, blood flow cycles all stages, 0 console errors.** Scripts `_v11c_vessels`/`_v11d_clean`/`_v11e_fixaorta`/`_export_v5`/`_render*`. **✅ v1.2 FOUNDER-FEEDBACK POLISH + BROWSER-VERIFIED 2026-06-21 (heart-v6.glb).** (a) **Rounder vessels** — re-converted curves at bevel_resolution 2→6 (~14 sides) + resolution_u 6→10 + smooth shading on all meshes (was faceted/hexagonal); now 73K polys / 3.05 MB (`_v12_vessels`/`_export_v6`). (b) **Taller canvas** — `minHeight: clamp(420px,62vh,680px)`. (c) **Zoom no longer snaps back** — root cause was drei `<Bounds observe>` continuously re-fitting whenever the canvas renders (and blood flow self-invalidates every frame → constant refit). **Replaced `<Bounds>` with a manual camera-frame effect** that runs only on load + real canvas resize (ResizeObserver), targets the off-origin model centre, and never per-frame; `frameloop="demand"` + blood-flow `state.invalidate()` self-loop. Verified in preview: heart fills the tall canvas, vessels are smooth tubes, and zoom HELD across multiple blood-flow segments. (d) **Model now served STATIC** from `apps/student/public/anatomy/heart-v6.glb` (no proxy hang; proxy route kept for future R2 uploads). **Lesson: a churned dev server (HMR) stalls the load — restart `npm run dev` if it sticks.** **✅ v1.3 GUIDED BLOOD-FLOW JOURNEY + BROWSER-VERIFIED 2026-06-21 (founder: "the dots just go in a circle, I'm clueless").** Rebuilt "Follow a drop of blood" from an ambient 14-particle swarm into a GUIDED tour of ONE drop: travels one leg at a time and PAUSES (~1.9s) at each of 8 stops; the current structure lights up emerald + gets an on-model name label; a big plain-English caption overlays the canvas ("Step 4 of 8 · Oxygen-poor blood — …"); the drop visibly lerps blue→red crossing the lungs and red→blue at the body. Per-stop highlight via new `highlightId` prop on HeartModel. **Bug fixed: `FLOW_STOPS` ids must be the CLASSIFIER structure ids (`svc`, `pulmonary_trunk`), not mesh tokens** — the swarm silently skipped those 2 stops; the guided drop got stuck at step 1 until fixed. Also: `frameloop` back to `bloodFlow?'always':'demand'` (demand+self-invalidate never started without a render kick; now safe for zoom since drei `observe` is gone). Browser-verified: journey advances 1→…→8, oxygen label flips at the lungs, on-model labels + captions render, zoom holds. **✅ v1.4 PLAYER CONTROLS + SEQUENCE STRIP + 16:9 + BROWSER-VERIFIED 2026-06-21.** Refactored blood flow to PARENT-CONTROLLED (React owns step/play/seek; `BloodFlow` just animates the drop `from`→`stop` on `animKey` bump, self-invalidates only ~1.8s per leg so `frameloop="demand"` again). Added **player controls** (‹ Prev · ❚❚ Pause/▶ Play · Next › · ↻ Replay-step) + a **clickable circuit sequence strip below the sim** (8 colour-coded chips blue→red, current highlighted, "· lungs +O₂ ·" marker, "↺ to body" loop hint; tap a chip → jump+pause). Canvas now **16:9** (`aspectRatio:'16/9'`, replaces the clamp height) and hub modal widened `max-w-4xl`→`max-w-6xl` for more canvas room. Browser-verified: 848×477 canvas, Pause freezes (step stayed across 5.5s), Next advances, chip-jump works, caption/chip/on-model-label all in sync. **✅ CANONICAL DOC WRITTEN 2026-06-21:** [`_agents/workflows/ANATOMY_3D_SIMULATOR_WORKFLOW.md`](_agents/workflows/ANATOMY_3D_SIMULATOR_WORKFLOW.md) — the full Z-Anatomy→Blender→glTF pipeline, viewer template, guided-flow pattern, design tokens, add-a-new-organ runbook + verification checklist + all gotchas. Cross-linked from `SIMULATION_DESIGN_WORKFLOW.md` + CLAUDE.md §6. Future organs (nephron/neuron/DNA) must follow it. **✅ BIOLOGY HUB SHIPPED 2026-06-21 (founder ask: a home for all bio sims, insertable into any grade's page).** Built on ONE shared catalog [biologySimulations.ts](packages/data/simulations/biologySimulations.ts) (`@canvas/data/simulations/*`; heart live + nephron/neuron/DNA placeholders): (a) **public hub** `apps/student/app/biology-hub/` (cacheable `revalidate=86400`; card grid + grade filter + modal that plays the sim via the public `BlockRenderer` — so the heart is now viewable WITHOUT placing a block); (b) **admin picker** — `SimulationBlockEditor` dropdown is now catalog-driven (friendly biology titles grouped on top); (c) refactor — extracted `EXTRA_SIMULATORS` to shared [extraSimulators.tsx](apps/student/features/books/lib/extraSimulators.tsx) so reader + hub share injected sims. Note: insertion into any Class 9–12 page already worked (sims are grade-agnostic); the hub adds discovery + a catalog + friendly insertion. tsc + eslint clean both apps. **✅ SKELETON — FIRST FULL BODY-SYSTEM SIM SHIPPED + BROWSER-VERIFIED 2026-06-21 (`skeleton-3d`, `skeleton-v4.glb`).** Z-Anatomy `1: Skeletal system` = 1,244 meshes (966 are zero-poly `.i/.j` label anchors, dropped) → **246 real parts (incl. teeth) merged into 22 tappable structures** (all skull bones + upper teeth→`skull`, lower teeth→`mandible`, 24 ribs→`ribs`, L+R limbs share a token; vertebral column split cervical/thoracic/lumbar/sacrum/coccyx so the NEET counts are tappable) — **246 draw calls → 22**. Decimated **per region** (skull + jaw NOT decimated at all = full-res symmetric face + real teeth with **roots trimmed to crowns** so they don't poke through the maxilla; ribs/scapula/hands/feet hard 0.11–0.15; limbs 0.20–0.45): **~506K base → 232K tris, 5.05 MB** uncompressed (no Draco; viewer `useGLTF(url,false)` like the heart). Principle: spend polys on the face (what learners zoom into), decimate hard where they don't. New viewer [Skeleton3DViewer.tsx](apps/student/features/anatomy/Skeleton3DViewer.tsx) copies all heart rules + adds an **Axial vs Appendicular toggle** (80/126 split, amber/sky highlight + fade), **5-region peel**, a guided **"Tour the skeleton"** step-through, a **"Name the bone" quiz**, and tap-to-label with NCERT blurbs. Registered in `extraSimulators.tsx` + catalog row in `biologySimulations.ts` (grades 9–12, LIVE → hub now shows "2 live"). Build scripts `scripts/blender/_skeleton_*.py` + `_glb_tris.js`. **Browser-verified:** renders framed+recognisable, axial/appendicular + peel + tour + quiz (scored 1/1) + tap-label all work, 0 console errors. **3 hard-won lessons (now in the canonical doc §4.7-4.8, §5.3):** (a) bones store their world position in a parent `.g` group, NOT their own transform → **capture `matrix_world` before deleting parents, then bake it into the verts** (else the whole skeleton collapses onto the origin — happened first try); (b) some Z-Anatomy bones carry a **SUBSURF modifier that `join()` inherits** → `export_apply` re-subdivides on export = 3–6× poly explosion (skull 69K→415K, file 13.6 MB) AND the aggressive decimation underneath mangled the facial bone (founder caught asymmetric orbits + maxilla spikes when zoomed in) → **`o.modifiers.clear()` before join/decimate**, and verify the EXPORTED glb tri count (`_glb_tris.js`), not Blender's `data.polygons`; (c) a tall subject needs a **TALL canvas** (`height:min(80vh,820px)`, not 16:9) + **`zoomToCursor`/`screenSpacePanning`** on OrbitControls so you can zoom into the skull/feet, not just centre (both founder feedback, fixed); (d) **the face is the inspection focus — do NOT decimate the skull/jaw** (collapse-decimation breaks L/R symmetry + shrinks bones apart at the sutures) and **KEEP the teeth but TRIM their roots to crowns** (excluding teeth → jagged empty sockets; whole teeth → long roots poke through the maxilla and read as spiky asymmetric teeth from an angle — the meshes ARE symmetric, verified per-tooth) — founder caught the face across **3 rounds** of close zoom; spend polys on the face, decimate hard on limbs/ribs. **✅ THE ACTUAL FACE BUG — NORMALS, not geometry (`skeleton-v5.glb`, 2026-06-21).** Founder: "in Blender it looks perfect" — the killer clue. Z-Anatomy builds every LEFT bone by mirroring its right twin, so the `.l` object's `matrix_world` has a **negative determinant**; baking it (`me.transform`) flips the winding → the whole left/face is inside-out. **Blender solid view is double-sided → perfect; our viewer is FrontSide → culls the flipped faces → "the right half looks broken/see-through"** (chased this as phantom geometry asymmetry for ~4 rounds). Fix: after baking, **if `world_mats[o].determinant() < 0` reverse the winding** (`bmesh.ops.reverse_faces`). Browser-verified: both halves now solid + symmetric. **Lesson: "perfect in Blender, broken in browser" == normals/winding, NOT geometry.** **✅ v2 INDIVIDUAL SKULL BONES (`skeleton-v6.glb`, 2026-06-21, founder ask "can't pinpoint individual bones, only the whole skull").** Un-merged the skull from 1 token into **13 tappable cranial+facial bones** (frontal, parietals, temporals, occipital, sphenoid, ethmoid; maxillae+upper teeth, zygomatics, nasals, lacrimals, palatines, inferior conchae, vomer) + mandible + hyoid — each with its own NCERT label/blurb. Now **37 nodes / 232K tris / 4.88 MB** (splitting only adds nodes, not polys). Updated classifier (both build stages), the export `TOKENS` allow-list (forgot it first → skull bones silently vanished from the glb), the viewer `STRUCTURES`, and the `QUIZ_POOL`/`TOUR` ids that referenced the old `skull` token. ## ⛔ Waiting-on-a-dependency (the things most easily forgotten)

These are *built or partly built* but stalled on something external. When the dependency lands, the linked work can resume — **this section exists so that "I forgot X was waiting on Y" can't happen.**

- **Adaptive Persona Engine → AI tutor needs Anthropic credit to go live.** Phase 2 (persona-snapshot + guardrailed AI tutor + model routing) is BUILT and verified up to the live model call. The live round-trip 400s on "credit balance too low" — the SDK wiring, API key, model id, and request shape all authenticate fine. **Action: top up the Anthropic account, then ask the agent to re-run the tutor live smoke test.** No code change needed.
- **Adaptive Persona Engine → cross-surface content dependency REMOVED (2026-06-07, ADR-012).** The engine no longer needs lecture/audio + `ResourceLink` rows to deliver value — Live Books are decoupled and the engine is re-centred on in-Crucible adaptive practice. The `ResourceLink` "read/watch this" path is **parked, not deleted** (dormant, revivable). The real remaining work is content-internal to Crucible: finish per-chapter micro-tagging + difficulty + self-sufficient solutions for 2–3 chemistry chapters, which then unblocks the **question grouping / variant-skipping** flagship (its own `variant_group_id` tag + design spec, TBD).
- **Class 9 Math Part II → needs NCERT PDFs** for DB chapters 9–15.
- **Class 9 English audio → needs recording** (schemas ready; all `audio_url` empty).
- **Crucible Physics/Maths micro-tagging → needs taxonomy micro_topics generated** for those subjects.
- **AI-Native Company (Canvas Brain) → any live LLM face needs Anthropic credit topped up** (same blocker as the Adaptive Persona tutor). The text-only brain substrate + founder advisor can be built and dry-run before the top-up; only the live answering call is blocked.
- **Live Books R2 hotlink protection (M-2) → needs the custom-domain migration.** The Worker + runbook are built (`workers/asset-hotlink-guard/`) and safe to deploy in log-only mode, but *enforcement* only bites once assets are served from a custom domain (`assets.canvasclasses.in`) instead of `pub-*.r2.dev` — which means a Cloudflare dashboard action (connect custom domain) **plus** moving `R2_PUBLIC_URL` + 204 hardcoded code refs + all DB-stored `book_pages`/notes URLs onto the new host (book-page rewrites MUST go through `book-writer.js`, §0.6). Until then the r2.dev host has no Worker and can't be protected. **Action: run `workers/asset-hotlink-guard/README.md` Phase 0 → 4.**
- **3D Anatomy Viewer (heart flagship) → Blender dependency RESOLVED 2026-06-21.** Blender 5.1.2 + the Blender Lab MCP add-on are installed and the server runs on `localhost:9876`; the agent drives it via the direct bridge `scripts/blender/blender_client.py` (the Claude Desktop connector itself wasn't reachable from the coding agent, so the bridge is the path). Phase B (import Z-Anatomy heart → name to `HEART_STRUCTURES` → decimate/colour → export `.glb` → compress → upload → set `HEART_MODEL_URL`) is now in progress.

## Standardised status header (for every plan/design doc)

So a project's detail doc tells you its state in 5 seconds, each `_agents/plans/*.md` carries this block at the top (the cockpit row is just its one-line summary):

```
> **Status:** 🟡 <one line> · **Last updated:** YYYY-MM-DD
> **Done:** <bullet or one line>
> **Pending:** <what's left>
> **Blocked on:** <dependency, or —>
> **Next action:** <single concrete next step>
```

`_agents/plans/UNIFIED_LEARNER_PERSONA.md` is the worked example.
